| Marcus distribution execution runtime | `src/services/marcusExecutionService.ts` — `runMarcusDistribution()` (agent phase 5, 2026-10-26): a readiness or review step gets `checkReadiness()` (`marcus/readiness.ts`, read-only: "Ready." or "Not ready:" with one line per point; asks nothing); a send step runs its saved preview through `runMarcusSend()`. One memory item per send (keys hidden) and a receipt. The keyword audit, `selectDistributionTarget`, the `#general` fallback and the second approval store are gone |
| Marcus's sends (agent phase 5) | `src/services/marcus/` (owner, 2026-10-26: his model picks where, you approve once with the exact text). `types.ts` (`Destination`, `SendPreview`, `PrepareResult`, `SendResult`); `destinations.ts` — `MARCUS_CONNECTORS` (Slack, Discord, Telegram (team chat `TELEGRAM_TEAM_CHAT_ID` or you), WhatsApp, GitHub (draft releases, issues), Notion, ClickUp, n8n (only workflows listed in `N8N_WORKFLOWS`), Composio), `destinationsFor()` (set up or not, with the sentence what to set up; defaults from Settings → Connectors' "Where Marcus sends" fields), `CHANNEL_LIMITS`, `isAllowedTarget()` (named in the request or a default; never made up). `sendText.ts` — `textToSend()` (payload text, else the step he sends from (`sourceStep()`: newest fix first, never a reviewer's), else one quoted piece). `pickDestination.ts` — a given destination is checked without the model; a connector the request names but that isn't set up says so; else Marcus's model picks, checked, one retry; Instagram/Facebook/YouTube → `CAMPAIGN_STUDIO_ONLY`; a release needs its version. `prepareSend.ts` — `prepareMarcusSend()` (too long for the channel → a fix through the review loop), `describeSend.ts` ("Send to Slack #news: “…”"). Quoted text (`src/services/jose/quotedContent.ts`: `withoutQuotedContent()`, `quotedPieces()`, shared with the router) is sent as you wrote it. The engine prepares a send step before its approval and saves `sendPreview` on the request step; the approval and Telegram show that line; `runSend.ts` — `runMarcusSend()` sends exactly the approved text once (`sentAtMs`), `WHATSAPP_24H`. `askMarcus.ts` — `askMarcusToSend()`: every send from outside a request (handoff packets, a workflow's Publishing task, old scheduled text sends) is one Marcus step; `src/test/marcusEntryPoints.test.js` fails when anything else calls `executeMarcusPublish(` or `executeComposioTool(`. Chat's `JoseRequestOutcome.tsx` shows "Now: …" once a waiting request ends elsewhere. Do NOT send for Marcus any other way Since QA batch 0 `isMarcusSendStep()` skips only Miya Studio's posting steps (they carry `contentStep`); a planned `distribution_schedule_posts` step without it is an ordinary send. Since QA batch 1 one named Slack or Discord with one named `#channel` is used without his model (`namedChannels()` in `pickDestination.ts`), and a model that doesn't answer is `MARCUS_MODEL_FAILED`, not `NO_DESTINATION`. `slackTokenProblem()` in `slackConnector.ts` refuses an app-level `xapp-` token on Save and in the Health Monitor. Slack goes through the backend (`sendSlackMessage()` → `connector_slack_send`): Slack refuses a bot token from the app window. A failed send waits (`setup_required` with `SEND_FAILED_CARRY_ON` in `marcusExecutionService.ts`) and any `publish_or_send` step runs once in the engine, no retries or larger model (owner, QA batch 1). |
| Timed sends (agent roles Part C2) | `src/services/marcus/sendTime.ts` — `readSendTime(text, nowMs)` (a time after a send word in the same sentence: `{ atMs }`; a bare today or tomorrow: `{ unclear: true }` → `SEND_TIME_UNCLEAR` asks for a day and time; none: null), `formatSendTime()` ("Tue 09:00", with the date beyond six days). `SendPreview.sendAtMs`; the approval reads "Send to X at Tue 09:00: “…”". Once approved, a send more than 60 s ahead is `scheduleApprovedSend()` in Marcus's runner (`kind: 'approved_send'`, claimed as `posting`, sent once; a `posting` item at start becomes `check`) and the step says "Marcus will send it to X at Y."; a time already past sends now and says so. On the Calendar through `listCalendarItems()`. Do NOT schedule a send any other way |
| Connector credential UI (all 9 API connectors) | `src/components/ConnectorSetupPanel.tsx` `CredentialSection` — saves via `saveConnectorCredential()` |
| Connector Health Monitor (Test / Validate) | `src/components/ConnectorHealthPanel.tsx` + `checkConnectorHealth()` in `connectorHealthCheckService.ts`. Every result says `checked: 'live'` (the service answered) or `'key_only'` (a saved key was found, nothing sent); the card shows "Live check passed" only for live, and each required key as "saved", "environment only" or "missing". Live checks: Telegram, WhatsApp gateway, GitHub, Slack, Discord, generic webhook, n8n, Hermes, NVIDIA NIM and Gemini (model lists), and Ollama and ComfyUI through the backend (both refuse the app window's origin). Do NOT show a presence check as "verified" (QA Bug 33, 2026-10-21). WhatsApp is Meta Cloud API only; Twilio support was removed (owner, 2026-10-21) |
| Saved connector keys at start | `connectorAuth.ts` — a credential read before the saved ones have loaded gets an empty placeholder that is NOT marked loaded and starts a load (until 2026-10-23 it was marked loaded, so every key looked missing for 60 s and the Telegram and WhatsApp companions, which check once at start, never started). A load that finds nothing saved counts as loaded. Test: `src/test/connectors/connectorAuthEarlyRead.test.js` |
| Using a saved connector key in backend commands | App side: `secretPresence()` / `savedSecret()` in `connectorOutbound.js` (saved key OR env var). Rust side: `utils::secret_or_env(passed, NAME)` — every connector command that needs a key takes it as an optional argument and falls back to the environment. Do NOT add a connector command that reads only `std::env::var`, or an app-side check that only calls `check_env_vars_presence`: keys saved in Settings live in the OS keychain, so such a path never sees them (QA Bug 32, 2026-10-21) |
| Telegram companion bot commands | `src/services/telegramCompanionService.js` — 21 commands: `/help`, `/report`, `/files`, `/status`, `/memory`, `/ping`, `/agents`, `/nova`, `/scan` + more. First-time `/start` owner pairing is gated on `TELEGRAM_ALLOWED_CHAT_IDS` (Settings → Connectors → Telegram, "required to pair") — do not remove this gate, it closes a real owner-registration race (Sprint 4). Calls the real Tauri commands `connector_poll_telegram`/`connector_send_telegram` (fixed 2026-07-10 — was calling nonexistent `telegram_get_updates`/`telegram_send_message`, silently swallowed). `processInboundCommands` uses `continue` (not `return`) per message in its loop so a full poll batch is processed, not just the first message (fixed 2026-07-10 — regression tests in `src/test/connectors/telegramCompanionService.test.js`). `/memory <query>` actually searches now (was dead code, shadowed by an earlier identical `if (cmd === 'memory')` branch) Since QA batch 1 it starts within 3 s of opening (`onIdle` in `useBootEffects.js` has a deadline) and `restartTelegramCompanion()` (Connectors → Telegram's Save & Enable) starts it with the token saved now. |
| WhatsApp companion bot commands | `src/services/whatsappCompanionService.ts` — 9 commands (`/status`, `/queue`, `/approve`, `/reject`, `/agents`, `/report`, `/ping`, `/help`, `/ask`) with real behavior, mirroring Telegram's pattern (added 2026-07-10 — WhatsApp previously had zero command handling, messages just landed in a passive inbox). Owner pairing gated on `WHATSAPP_ALLOWED_NUMBERS` (same security pattern as Telegram's `TELEGRAM_ALLOWED_CHAT_IDS`). Auto-starts at boot via `useBootEffects.js` if `WHATSAPP_ACCESS_TOKEN` + `WHATSAPP_CLOUD_GATEWAY_DRAIN_URL` are both configured — previously WhatsApp inbound polling only ran via a manual "Poll" button in the Orchestrator view, never automatically. `WHATSAPP_CLOUD_GATEWAY_DRAIN_URL` and `WHATSAPP_ALLOWED_NUMBERS` credential fields added to `ConnectorSetupPanel.tsx` (the drain URL field didn't exist anywhere in the UI before — inbound polling was unconfigurable regardless of other credentials) |
| Voice STT pipeline | `src/services/voiceService.ts` + `src/hooks/useVoiceInput.js` — SpeechRecognition with fallback |
| Voice OS sidecar launcher | `src-tauri/src/voice_sidecar.rs` — `voice_start`/`voice_stop`/`voice_status` Tauri commands. Runs uvicorn on **port 8766** (moved from 8765 on 2026-07-10 — collided with the Mobile Companion WebSocket server, which also defaulted to 8765; iOS hardcodes 8765 in Swift so Voice OS moved instead). `runtime_manager.rs`'s `voice-os` ToolDef (a second, independent launch path via Runtime Hub) updated to match — keep both in sync if the port ever changes again. `voice_start`'s Python interpreter resolution (`resolve_voice_python()`, added 2026-07-10) checks Runtime Hub's install location (`runtimes_dir()/voice-os/venv` — the only place anything ever `pip install`s Voice OS's dependencies) before falling back to a bundled-resource venv or bare system `python` — do not revert to checking only the bundled-resource path, that was the root cause of Voice OS never actually starting even after a successful Runtime Hub install. |
| Voice OS React service | `src/services/voiceOsService.ts` — `startVoiceServer`, `stopVoiceServer`, `getVoiceServerStatus`. `DEFAULT_WS_URL` is `ws://127.0.0.1:8766/ws`. The watchdog (`startVoiceWatchdog()` / `checkVoiceWatchdog()`, every 30 s from App.tsx) restarts Voice OS only after it has seen it running, never after a deliberate Stop, and gives up after 5 tries; until 2026-10-22 it treated "never started" as crashed and showed "Voice OS offline — restarting..." five times to every user who doesn't use voice (QA Bug 77) |
| Voice OS control panel (sidebar page) | `src/components/VoiceView.tsx` — real status (polled from `voice_status`), Start/Stop, WS URL once running, Python-prerequisite warning. Wired at `App.tsx`'s `activeTab === 'voice'` (added 2026-07-10 — the "Voice" sidebar nav item existed since before, but nothing ever rendered for it; the page was blank). Do NOT create another voice control view. |
| Jarvis voice hook | `src/hooks/useJarvisVoice.ts` — AudioWorklet WebSocket voice hook (start/stop/reset/state/transcript/reply/activeAgent/error/isConnected) |
| PCM worklet (required) | `src/hooks/pcm-processor.worklet.ts` — `PCM_WORKLET_CODE` string constant imported by `useJarvisVoice.ts`. Do NOT remove — voice breaks without it. |
| Voice OS backend | `voice/backend/` — FastAPI STT→LLM→TTS pipeline (faster-whisper, piper, webrtcvad, real Ollama `/api/chat` streaming call via `_call_ollama` in `pipeline.py`, VAD-gated via `vad.py`'s `is_speech()`). `pipeline.py` was fundamentally broken until 2026-07-10 — it called a nonexistent `llm` function with the conversation-history list passed as if it were callable (crashed every request after transcription), never `await`ed the async `synthesize()` call, and had no VAD gate at all. Fully rewritten to match the pre-existing `tests/test_pipeline.py` contract; 31/31 pytest passing (was 28/31 including 3 unrelated router.py bugs, also fixed same pass). Piper TTS voice model (`en_US-lessac-medium.onnx`) now auto-downloads on first use if missing (`tts.py`) instead of silently producing empty audio forever. Since 2026-10-22: audio is collected into whole utterances by `UtteranceCollector` (`vad.py`: speech, then a 0.7 s pause, 15 s at most) before anything is transcribed (it used to cut every 1.5 s and the silence after a question cancelled its answer); the Ollama model comes from the app's `{"type": "config", "ollamaModel"}` message (sent by `useJarvisVoice` on connect), else `VOICE_OLLAMA_MODEL`, else `llama3.2:3b`; the Ollama address only from `LOCAL_OLLAMA_URL`, which `voice_start` sets from Settings (`startVoiceServer()` passes `ollamaEndpoint`/`ollamaModel`, checked by `voice_ollama_chat_url()` / `voice_ollama_model()` in `voice_sidecar.rs`). Do NOT accept an address over the Voice OS connection: CodeQL flagged that as server-side request forgery (2026-10-22). Runtime Hub's Start for Voice OS passes neither, so it uses localhost; speech recognition from `VOICE_STT_MODEL` / `VOICE_STT_LANGUAGE` (defaults `tiny.en`, English). The installer bundles only `voice/backend/*.py` and `requirements.txt` (not a developer's `venv`). Python, the libraries (about 300 MB) and the speech models are NOT in the installer: Runtime Hub installs them. |
| Workflow visual builder UI | `src/components/WorkflowBuilderView.tsx` — two-panel editor, 9 node types, reorder, save |
| Workflow run panel (agent-chain workflows) | `src/components/WorkflowPanel.tsx` — lists/runs `workflowRegistryService.ts`'s agent-chain workflows (`listWorkflows`/`runWorkflow`/`executeWorkflowTasks`). Since phase 6a `runWorkflow()` runs the chain as given steps through `submitStepsToJose()` (`workflowSteps()`: one step per agent after Jose, each needing the one before, task types from `WORKFLOW_STEP_TASK`; Marcus reviews and sends nothing; label `WORKFLOW_LABEL`); the panel's Run and the phone's run_workflow still call `executeWorkflowTasks()` (the operations executors, not an agent request); a third, distinct workflow system alongside `WorkflowBuilderView.tsx` (visual node graphs) and `AutomationView.tsx`'s Operations tab (governed templates via `workflowOperationsRegistryService.ts`) — see `workflowExecutionService.js`'s own header comment for how the three relate Since Jose phase 7 the panel's Run and the phone's run_workflow call `runWorkflow()` too (a Jose request); `src/test/joseEntryPoints.test.js` fails when a screen or App calls `executeWorkflowTasks(`. |
| Nova insight card | `src/components/nova/NovaVerdictCard.tsx`, rendered by `ChatView.tsx` (`novaCommandId`) — since agent phase 2 (2026-10-25) it shows Nova's saved verdict for the request (what to do first and her top three) only when the request had a Nova step; no extra model call. Until then it scored your own message by keywords |
| Sentinel card in the sidebar | `src/components/RightPanel.tsx` — Security section: Sentinel's last review of the week (`lastSentinelReview()`, read from its request: Safe green, Risky amber, Unsafe red, with his findings), refreshed every 5 s on the System tab. Re-scan asks for a new review (`askSentinelWeekReview(recentActivityText(7).text)`); nothing starts on its own. Since agent phase 4 (2026-10-25); before, a keyword scan whose colours read a field it never returned, so a critical result showed green |

Sends are Marcus's channel (`marcus/composioChannel.ts`); read-only actions are the `composio_read` skill tool (Hector, Echo, Alphonso; refuses send tools). Alphonso's automatic Composio path and `use_composio_tool` are gone |
| Hector's research (agent phase 1) | `src/services/hector/` (2026-10-25, owner: "a research task should come back as a proper result, not just a Google search"): `researchRun.ts` — `runResearch(question, { request, focus, urls, large, reportId })`: `planQuestion()` (Hector's model breaks the question into at most 5 parts with up to 3 short queries each; unreadable → `fallbackQuery()`, the request's own words without `/jose`, the HELP line or Jose's notes, ≤150 characters), a search round, reading, a write-up with numbered citations (`parseWriteUp()`: findings citing a source that doesn't exist are dropped; support computed from the sources: several / one / one, not primary), then follow-up rounds for parts still `partly`/`open` (a part counts as answered only when a finding cites a page Hector read, never a snippet alone; a planned part the write-up leaves out comes back open) (2 for a local model under the tool-call size, 3 otherwise; stops early), never re-reading a source; nothing usable → `{ ok: false, reason }` (no facts from memory); `reportText()` the hand-over: the answer first, parts with cited findings, disagreements, what wasn't found, numbered sources labelled Read / Snippet only / Couldn't read. `searchChain.ts` — the one search chain for every caller: Brave, Tavily, Perplexity (each when set up), DuckDuckGo through the backend, news feeds; junk dropped, ranked by `sourceQuality.ts` (`isJunkResult()`: home, search, sign-in pages and walls; `sourceKind()`: primary by domain suffix or paper path, news by a dated path; no site lists). DeepSeek is no longer a source. `relevantText.ts` (paragraphs matching the question's word stems, menus dropped, budget 8,000 / 24,000 characters per write-up). `researchLimits.ts` (`HECTOR_LIMITS`). `hectorStep.ts` — `runHectorStep()`, Jose's Hector step: the cited report as `summary` and `schema`, `setup_required` with the reason when nothing usable, `failed` when the model writes no report; reuses a Research Desk report given as `payload.reportId`. `deskRequest.ts` — `askOnResearchDesk(reportId)`: the Desk's Run is a one-step request through `submitStepsToJose()` (`DESK_LABEL`). Backend: `fetch_research_sources` asks like a browser, reads at most 2 MB of a page (`read_capped()`, agent phase 4), skips a disguised link ("looks disguised (…)") or one Google Web Risk lists, both listed after the cited sources, (`research_client()`: a desktop user agent, the app's languages), retries once on 403/429/503 (`fetch_page_with_retry()`), keeps a PDF as its search snippet (`is_pdf()`); `src-tauri/src/research_store.rs` keeps page text in `<app data>/research/<reportId>/<n>.txt` (`research_save_pages`, `research_read_page`, `research_delete`). Reports in app storage keep excerpts of 600 characters; 200 kept, a dropped report's pages deleted; `slimOldReports()` moves older reports' page text to files once at start. Screens: `ResearchFindings.tsx` (on the Desk and the board), `SavedSources.tsx`, `HectorApprovalHandoff.tsx` now "Use in a request" (copies the cited report). Skill tools: `read_sources` (numbered excerpts), `read_source_page`, `read_bookmarks`. Do NOT search with the step text or add another search path Since agent roles Part C2: the backend reads a PDF source's text (`pdf_text()` in `search.rs`, `pdf-extract` 0.12.1, off the async thread, 20 s, 200,000 characters, parser panics caught; `pdfTextRead`), else it keeps its snippet with "couldn't read the PDF's text"; a search result is read only when it shares two of the question's words; his skill's instructions go first in his write-up prompt; the PowerPoint export shows a newer report's answer, cited parts and labelled sources. |
| Task types with meanings; the kind-of-work check (agent phase 1) | `src/services/jose/taskTypeMeanings.ts` — `TASK_TYPE_MEANINGS` (every task type in use with a plain meaning; a test fails when one is missing), `taskTypesFor(agent)`, `planningMenu()` (what Jose's planner sees per agent, instead of bare prefixes), `isCodeRequest(text)` (since agent phase 6: code words, languages, code file names, your app/project/repo, making an app or site, running the tests, scripts on files; test, check, run, add, install, file or verify alone are not code), `needsFacts(text)`, `CODE_TASK_PREFIXES`, `isCodeTask()`. `checkPlan(steps, { request })` refuses a code step for a request that isn't about code ("<step> is writing, not code: give it to Alphonso as assistant_write."); only plans Jose's model writes pass `request`. | Hermes Agent connector (per-agent LLM backend) | `src/services/connectors/hermesAgentConnector.ts` — talks to the user's own separately-run standalone Hermes Agent instances (Nous Research, MIT), one live OpenAI-compatible profile per in-app agent. `isHermesAgentConfigured(agentId)`, `listHermesAgentModels(agentId)` (`GET /v1/models`), `getHermesAgentHealth(agentId)` (unauthenticated `GET /health`), `sendHermesAgentMessage(agentId, messages, {model, approved, sessionId})` (`POST /v1/chat/completions`). Credentials stored per agent under the `hermes_agents` connector id (`HERMES_<AGENTID>_URL`/`_KEY`/`_SESSION_MODE` keys via the existing flat `connectorAuth.ts` store — no bespoke JSON blob). Registered in `connectorRegistry.js`'s `DEFAULT_CONNECTORS`; deliberately excluded from `PAID_OR_METERED_CONNECTORS` in `policyEnforcementService.ts` (local/self-hosted, same posture as Ollama) but classified **high risk** in `classifyConnectorRisk` (2026-08-21, Phase 1b — any call can trigger real tool use, not just text generation). Credential UI: `HermesAgentsSection` in `ConnectorSetupPanel.tsx` — one row per agent from `agentRegistry.js`, never hardcoded. Design doc (gitignored, machine-local): `docs/HERMES_AGENT_DELEGATION_PLAN.md`; tracked in `docs/TRUTH_FIRST_EXECUTION_PLAN.md` §I. **Phase 1b hardening (CLOSED 2026-08-21):** circuit breaker (`connectorCircuitBreakerService.ts` gained a per-connector `configure()` API for this), rate limiter tuned for localhost traffic, every call path audited via `appendConnectorAudit`, health-check panel registration (`checkHermesAgentsConnection` — aggregates across all configured agents since Hermes is per-agent unlike every other connector), and session continuity (`getHermesSessionMode`/`setHermesSessionMode`, `X-Hermes-Session-Id` header, wired into every per-agent call site as of 2026-10-06 — Boardroom via `activeThreadId`; Jose's Miya/Hector/Alphonso(brain) via `assignment.packetId`; Maria/Echo/Nova/Sentinel via their `assignment.packetId`; `echoFileWatcherService` via `echo-watch:<relativePath>`; Hector synthesis via the report id; `CompanionMode` via `companion-mode:<agentId>`; a call with no natural id passes none rather than a fabricated one; guarded by `src/test/hermesSessionContinuity.test.js`). `sendHermesAgentMessage` now returns a structured `{blocked:true}`/`{circuitOpen:true}` result instead of throwing on a policy-gate block (matches every other connector's convention) — `generateAgentLlmResponse` still throws for its 9 existing callers, so no behavior change there. **Approval-flow gap CLOSED same day (2026-08-21):** Jose's Miya/Hector builders get `isBlockedByHermesApproval(assignment)` in `joseExecutionEngineService.ts`'s wave loop (mirrors the Zero-Cost Mode / Sentinel gates exactly — routes to `pending_approval`, resumed via the existing `executeApprovedPackets`/`ApprovalPanel` flow); Boardroom (`BoardroomChatView.tsx`) calls the existing `requestApproval()` bridge from `App.tsx` directly (passed down as a prop from `MissionRoomBoardroomTabs` — the first component to receive anything from `App.tsx`, done as a prop rather than a reverse import since no component previously imported *from* `App.tsx`). Both reuse the one shared approval primitive rather than a second mechanism. **Correction 2026-10-06:** Boardroom's gate went through `App.tsx`'s `RequestApprovalProvider` bridge, which previously never displayed a prompt (it awaited forever); the bridge now forwards to the shell's real approval modal via `registerApprovalHandler`, and Boardroom passes an object with `requireApproval: true` (see `docs/governance/DEFERRED_WORK.md`, 2026-10-06). |

| Maria's reviews (agent phase 3) | `src/services/maria/` (2026-10-25, owner: model judgment only, two kinds of specific answer). `mariaTypes.ts` — `MariaStepResult`, `MariaAssignment` and `MARIA_SECURITY_LINE`, shared by both reviews. `mariaWorkReview.ts` — `runMariaWorkReview()` for a step with `reviewOf`: pass/fix with findings `{quote, problem, change, file?, line?}`; every quote must be in the work (`parseWorkReview()`; one retry "Quote the words you mean. Copy them exactly as they are in the work.", then `MARIA_NO_REVIEW`), claims not backed by a source make it `fix`; schema `{ kind: 'work', verdict, findings (strings), review, summary, riskLevel, approvalRequired }`; summary starts "Pass." or "Fix:". She reads what the work was built from (`workContext.ts`: `workSteps()` nearest needs first, `workSources()`, and for Alphonso's work his files (`read_workspace_file`, 6,000 / 20,000 characters, `FILE_LIMITS`) and validation results, reviewed with file:line; since agent phase 6 read from the folder he worked in, `reviewedFolder()` in `alphonso/stepFolder.ts`). `mariaPlanReview.ts` — `runMariaPlanReview()`: risk low/medium/high and why, the rules that apply, points, what needs your approval (only with reasons from her points or rules; low with approval is refused once, then medium), and an offer; summary starts `Risk: <level>.`; schema `{ kind: 'plan', … riskLevel, approvalRequired, summary }`. Security goes to Sentinel through a `help` field that becomes a `HELP:` line (`MARIA_SECURITY_LINE`; `ruleHelper()` in `joseHelp.ts` sends security words to Sentinel `security_review`). `mariaHolds.ts` — `mariaHoldFor()` / `holdStepsAfter()`: when she asks for approval, or her last re-review still says fix after `JOSE_LIMITS.maxFixRounds`, every step after hers waits (`waitFor`: "Maria asks for your approval: …" / "Maria asked for changes: …"), applied by the engine after her output is saved. Marcus reads her review of the exact work he sends (`mariaReviewFor(commandId, needs)` in `marcusExecutionService.ts`), with no keyword score (`generateRiskScore` no longer used there) and no block of his own. `mariaWeekly.ts` — `askMariaWeeklyRisks(work)`: the Monday schedule ("Maria: the week's risks") as one Maria step (`MARIA_WEEKLY_LABEL`); App no longer starts `mariaWeeklyReportService`. Chat: `src/components/maria/MariaOfferButton.tsx` ("Have Marcus prepare this": one Marcus `distribution_readiness` step, `read` only, `MARIA_OFFER_LABEL`). Content quality reviews compare with what is known about the brand's references (`referenceKnowledge()` in `contentStepPrompts.ts`: Hector's title, hook and why it works, else only the link and note; `comparedTo` names one or "no reference known"), and their verdict comes from her scores against the brand's threshold and her honesty issues (`contentQualityVerdict()` in `joseReviewLoop.ts`). She judges only what she sees, never another agent's check she didn't see (agent phase 4) |
| Sentinel's checks (agent phase 4) | `src/services/sentinel/` (2026-10-25, owner: "his model, plus exact checks"). `exactChecks.ts` — `findExactRisks(text, { trustedDomains?, links: 'all' | 'sent' })` → facts `{ kind, where, detail }` (`key`: sk-, ntn_, ghp_, github_pat_, xox*, AKIA, Bearer, NAME=value, PEM private keys and the `[hidden key, ends …XXXX]` placeholder; `password_request`; `disguised_link`: a bare IP, punycode, digits in place of letters in a site asking you to log in, a login over http, link text showing another domain; `untrusted_link` only for text being sent; `deleting_command` / `code_command` from `deletesThings()` / `isWorkspaceCodeExecuting()`), never a score; `checkLink(url)`; `redactText(text)` (`[hidden key, ends …efgh]`, used by `buildStepInput()` for every step's request and instruction, so no agent's model sees a key); `exactRiskText()`. `sentinelReview.ts` — `runSentinelReview(assignment, input, { commandId, workspaceRoot })` (his step, every Sentinel task type; `runSentinelSecurityScan()` calls it): checks the `reviewOf` work, else a `payload.text` handed to Marcus, else the needed steps' work, else the request; for Alphonso's work his files, results and dependency files (`workDependencyFiles()` in `maria/workContext.ts`), from the folder he worked in (agent phase 6); security-only prompt (`sentinelPrompt()`), page text fenced, keys hidden; answer `{ verdict: safe/risky/unsafe, summary, findings: [{ what, where, severity, fix }] }`, quotes checked against the hidden text (one retry), contradictions retried then `risky`, every exact fact added as a finding, unreadable twice → fails with `SENTINEL_NO_CHECK` and the facts; Jose's step model; schema `{ kind: 'security', verdict, findings, summary, severity, requiresApproval, reviewVerdict (pass/fix with reviewOf, read first by `judgeReview()`) }`; hand-over starts "Safe." / "Risky:" / "Unsafe:"; one memory item, keys hidden. `sentinelHolds.ts` — `sentinelHoldFor(assignment, result, maxRounds)`: a high finding or unsafe holds every later step ("Sentinel asks for your approval: …", through `holdStepsAfter()`), so does a fix left after the rounds and an issue in the content factory's campaign check (`SECURITY_WAIT`, so Publish waits). `addSecurityChecks.ts` — `addSecurityChecks(steps, request)`, applied by `submitToJose()` and `submitStepsToJose()` before `checkPlan()`: `sentinel-send-<step>` before every Marcus send (reviews the step he sends from), `sentinel-code-<step>` after every Alphonso step that changes or runs code, `sentinel-research-<step>` before an acting step that needs Hector, `sentinel-request` first when the request has a key or a link (`KEY_IN_REQUEST` in the plan note); never twice. The command approval window lists "Sentinel: deletes files / runs code: <command>". `fence.ts` — `fenced()`, `UNTRUSTED_RULE`: tool results (`skillGather.ts`, `skillSection()`) and Hector's work in the next agent's input are marked untrusted page text. `webRisk.ts` + `src-tauri/src/web_risk.rs` — optional Google Web Risk lookup (`web_risk_check`, key `WEB_RISK_API_KEY` in Connectors → Search & Research; Google's free Safe Browsing API is for non-commercial use only; Web Risk's first 100,000 checks a month are free, then $0.50 per 1,000; only the address is sent, answers kept 30 minutes, the key never in an error); `checkLinksWithWebRisk(urls)` never throws. Screens: `src/components/sentinel/LinkWarning.tsx` (`LinkWarningHost` in App: Open anyway / Cancel for a disguised or listed source link, through `setLinkConfirmHandler()`; no window → the link doesn't open), Campaign Studio's "Checked by Sentinel". The keyword scanner, its fallback and the engine's keyword gate are gone; `sentinelGateService.ts` has no caller (DEFERRED_WORK). Do NOT score from keywords or show a scan of nothing Since agent roles Part C2: `findExactRisks(text, { personalData: true })` adds `personal_data` facts (private emails, phone numbers, street addresses, long ID numbers; high), on for `security_personal_data`, which `addSecurityChecks()` adds as `sentinel-people-<step>` after every Hector `research_people` step. Since Jose phase 7 the content factory's Publish gets no `sentinel-send-` check (its own campaign check covers it; CI found the extra check blocking Publish with no model). |

the old `settings.workspaceRoot` becomes the first project), `projectNamedIn()` (whole words, longest wins), `scratchPlace()` (`<output folder>/<agentName('alphonso')>/<slug>/`, `-2`… when taken; `NEEDS_OUTPUT_FOLDER`), `projectPlace()`, `getCodeModel()` (`settings.alphonsoCodeModel`, "Same as Alphonso" when empty; nothing is picked from a model's name), `NO_PROJECT`. `projectLock.ts` — `claimProject(folder, requestId)` (one request per folder; stale after `JOSE_LIMITS.projectMs`), `releaseProject()`, `onProjectReleased()` (the engine's `wakeWaitingFor()` carries on the oldest request waiting for that folder), `busyLine()`. `readProject.ts` — `listProjectFiles()` over the backend's `list_project_files` (`workspace.rs`, the `ignore` crate: `.gitignore` respected, `.git` skipped) and `readProjectFiles()` (`FILE_LIMITS`, `STEP_INPUT_LIMITS`). `changeSet.ts` — `classifyFile()` (new/changed, +/− lines, first changed lines, `contentHash()`), `classifyCommand()` (`assessCommandRisk()`, read-only runs without asking), `previewNeeds()` (change_project / run / delete), `describeCodeChange()` ("Change 2 files in shop (src/App.tsx +12 −3) …"). `codeStep.ts` — `isAlphonsoCodeStep()` (an Alphonso step with a `CODE_TASK_PREFIXES` task type, not a content step), `parseCodeAnswer()`. `prepareCode.ts` — `prepareAlphonsoCode()`: the place (saved choice, named project, his model's answer, one project, else `WHICH_PROJECT` with `projectChoices`), the lock, the listing (`FOLDER_GONE`), the files he asks to read, then his model's change (Settings code model through `withAgentModel`); unsafe paths dropped with a reason (`safePath()`: no drive letters, absolute paths, `.git` or names of three or more dots; fix rounds use it too); a command the command policy would refuse (the read-only backend command `command_allowed`, the same `command_policy_refusal()` that `execute_command_verified` uses) is sent back once with the reason, then left out with it; a command written as one string is split and "None" dropped (`parseCodeAnswer()`); with several projects and none named, a project his model picks counts as unknown, so he asks; help through `HELP:`; never throws (`MODEL_DIDNT_ANSWER`). Called by the engine before the approval gate; the step waits with `codePreview` and one approval for exactly its `previewNeeds()`. `runCode.ts` — `runAlphonsoCode()` after approval (a refused command is a failed run with the reason; on Windows `npm`/`npx`/`yarn`/`pnpm` start as their `.cmd` files, `spawn_name()` in `commands/system.rs`): refuses a file changed since the preview, writes only approved or new paths, runs the commands in the folder (`verifyCommandExecution`), up to `JOSE_LIMITS.maxFixRounds` fix rounds, a plain summary, `code_file` / `command_result` artifacts, one memory item; runs once (no retries or escalation). `stepFolder.ts` — `stepFolder()` / `reviewedFolder()`: Maria's and Sentinel's reviews read the folder he worked in. `answerPrompt.ts` — `ALPHONSO_ANSWER_PROMPT` (Chat's and channels' answers with the last 6 messages, his answering steps; Direct Mode keeps `CHAT_ASSISTANT_PROMPT`). Git and dev servers only when asked. plain reasons), `skillTools.ts` (`SKILL_TOOLS`, `ACTION_TOOLS`, `toolsFor()`), `skillToolRun.ts` (`runSkillTool()` wraps existing functions; action tools (send, publish, call, run, change files, make media) never run there: they are the step's own action, after its approval), `skillLibrary.ts` (`listSkills()`, `skillsForTask()`, `refusedSkills()`, `setUserSkillFiles()`, `contentAsk()`: the content factory's asks live in its skills now), `skillPick.ts` (`pickSkill()`: only skills for the step's task are offered; | Command risk rating | `assessCommandRisk(program, args)` in `src/services/commandApprovalService.ts` (2026-10-22) — rates a command from what it does: only reads → low and `readOnly` (runs without asking); changes files/packages/history/models or contacts the internet → medium; runs project code, deletes, or an unknown program → high; with plain-language `reasons`. Used by `verifyCommand` (Operator Dashboard) and AI-planned command approval; the approval window lists the reasons and shows a number only when one was measured (`mariaScore`). Do NOT pass a fixed `riskLevel: 'high'` for a command or turn a level into a number: every command used to show "85". It only decides whether to ask; `policy_gate.rs` decides what may run |
| Approval for AI-planned workspace-code commands | `src/services/commandApprovalService.ts` — `isWorkspaceCodeExecuting(program,args)` (npm/yarn/pnpm run/test/build/install, npx except tsc, cargo build/check/test/run, `python -m pytest`/pip install, and any unknown program count as code-executing; git/rustc/ollama/version-only/`py_compile` do not), `requestWorkspaceCommandApproval(commands,{source,projectDir,cache})`, `setCommandApprovalHandler()`. Registered once in `App.tsx` with the shell's `requestApproval`, so it reuses the existing approval modal and honors Approval Mode; **fail-closed** (no handler = denied). Gates both `parsed.commands` and the automatic post-write validation (`npm run build`/`lint`) in `agentBrainService.js` (a denied validation yields a `validation_skipped` artifact, never a false "passed"). `chatUtils.js`'s `needsHighRiskApproval` carries the `run workspace code` term the label depends on — keep them in sync. Since agent phase 6 Alphonso's code steps no longer read build/test/install intents from the request: his model proposes the commands, read-only ones run, and the rest are in the step's preview and approved once with it (`src/services/alphonso/`, row above). Same pass blocked writes into `.git/` (`sanitizeRelativePath` + Rust `rel_touches_git_dir`), since hooks/`core.fsmonitor` there execute during ordinary git commands. Since 2026-10-23 a command that deletes (`deletesThings()`: git reset --hard, clean, force push, branch -d, ollama rm, cargo clean) is always gated and asked with `alwaysAsk`, which `useAppShellState.requestApproval` honours even with Approval Mode off; code-running commands ask only while Settings → Approvals "Run code and commands" is on (`reason: 'not_required_by_settings'` otherwise). Do NOT add a second command-approval mechanism. |

Jose's own steps (`executeJoseStep()`) do no work: his `cost_policy_` approval gate finishes without a model, anything else fails "No agent owns this step's task". Until 2026-10-24 Jose answered these himself; until 2026-10-22 it returned "Jose completed orchestration review" without asking any model (QA Bug 68). | CALL-E conversational MCP outreach (Phase 2) | `src/services/calleMcpAuthService.ts` (`startBrokerLogin`/`pollBrokerLogin`/`getCalleMcpToken`/`isCalleMcpConfigured`/`disconnectCalleMcp` — poll-based brokered OAuth against `seleven-mcp-sg.airudder.com`, no loopback server needed; token stored via `secureStorageService.ts`) + `src/services/connectors/calleMcpConnector.ts` (`planCall`/`runCall`/`getCallRun` — plain-`fetch` MCP JSON-RPC client, no SDK dependency, matches this repo's zero-SDK-per-connector convention; unwraps the MCP `CallToolResult` envelope via `structuredContent`/`content[].text` before returning) + `src/services/calleMcpOutreachService.ts` (`handleMcpOutreachMessage`/`confirmMcpOutreachCall`/`cancelMcpOutreachCall`/`recoverInterruptedMcpOutreachCalls` — clarifying-question state machine, durable via `kv_store.rs`, a `submitting` stage persisted before `runCall` since that tool has no idempotency key, a `confirmInFlight` double-submit guard, and a bounded `MAX_POLL_DURATION_MS` poll ceiling). Wired into `ChatView.tsx` (a real button-click approval via `pendingConfirmChatId`, not a typed "confirm") and `ConnectorSetupPanel.tsx`'s `CalleMcpConnectionBlock`. Distinct from the Phase 1 REST connector above — this is the conversational path where the user describes a call in chat and CALL-E's own `plan_call`/`run_call`/`get_call_run` MCP tools drive clarification and execution, rather than a form-driven draft. **Schema live-verified 2026-09-07** against a real authenticated `tools/list` + a real planning-only `plan_call` round trip via the official `calle` CLI (skills.sh skill), which corrected two real design mismatches: `plan_call` has no `conversation_history` param at all (state is tracked server-side via the opaque `plan_id`, threaded through with each turn's raw `user_input`), and the response never echoes a phone number (`phone_number` doesn't exist — the original cross-chat same-phone dedup check was silently dead code, removed rather than left in place; per-chat duplicate-submission protection still holds via `confirmInFlight` + the `submitting` stage). See `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-06-calle-mcp-conversational-outreach-design.md` for full design + self-critique history (documents real bugs found and fixed, incl. a chat-thread-monopolization bug and the storage-layer/idempotency issues). |

Uses Perplexity's Search API (`POST /search`, ranked results with title/url/snippet/date) since 2026-10-21; the old `/chat/completions` call used a retired model, and Perplexity ended Sonar Chat Completions support on 2026-09-27. Third in Hector's one search chain (`searchChain.ts`), after Tavily. Not yet live-tested (no key) |

Wan 2.2 14B was ruled out (two 14 GB halves). `parse_gpu_memory_mib()` / `gpu_memory_gb()` (`nvidia-smi --query-gpu=memory.total`), `suggest_video_model()` (the largest that fits; none without an NVIDIA card), `comfyui_video_models(savedDir)` and `comfyui_install_video_model(savedDir, id)` (into the user's ComfyUI `models/<subfolder>/` through `download_verified()`, progress as tool `comfyui-video`). App side: `src/services/videoModelService.ts` (`listVideoModels`, `installVideoModel`, `get/setSelectedVideoModel` (key `alphonso_comfyui_video_model_v1`, null = the suggestion), `effectiveVideoModel`) and `src/components/VideoModelsPanel.tsx` on Runtime Hub's ComfyUI card. `download_verified()` in `runtime_manager.rs` takes a `DownloadNote` (label and progress band) since phase 2b; it used to say "the starter image model" for Kokoro's model too |
| The AI clips step (content factory phase 2b) | `clips` in `STEP_ORDER` between `broll` and `assemble` (Miya, `creative_video_clips`, `write_own_work`; the Content video template has 16 steps then, 17 with Publish since phase 3a, 18 with Carousel since phase 3c, 19 with Shorts since phase 3d, 20 with Sound effects since phase 4b; `assemble` needs `clips`). `contentProjectPlanSteps(…, { clipEngine })`: when AI clips are on, a project run waits at clips (`CLIPS_WAIT` = "Check the main moments in Miya Studio first.", or `CLIPS_WAIT_PAID` with the Runway cost); a Runway run adds `spend` (one approval per run); run on its own from Miya Studio it doesn't wait, but Runway still asks. `checkPlan()` lets only Miya's `creative_video_clips` step spend besides paid-model steps. `runContentProject()`/`runContentStep('clips')` plan with `resolveProjectClipEngine()` (`contentMediaExecutor.ts`: the effective video model from `videoModelService`, `runwayClipAvailable()`). `approveContentStep(projectId, 'clips')` approves the waiting clips step (the step itself has no approval status). `runClips()` reads the marks when it runs, per scene: no picture → skipped; local `makeComfyClip()` (at most the model's length) or Runway `makeRunwayClip()` (only with `spend` on the assignment, else `RUNWAY_NOT_APPROVED`; the monthly cap stops the rest); output `{ engine, model, clips: [{ sceneId, status, path, seconds, costUsd, jobId, reason }], totalUsd, note }`; off → `{ skipped: CLIPS_OFF }` |
| AI clip settings and main moments (content factory phase 2b) | `src/services/contentFactory/clipEngine.ts` — `resolveClipEngine(settings, { localModelInstalled, runway })` (a chosen engine, or automatic: local if a video model is installed, else Runway, else none with `NO_CLIP_ENGINE`), `chosenScenes(scenes, aiClips)`. `contentTypes.ts`: `VideoSettings` (`aiClips` main/every/none, `clipEngine` local/runway/null, `runwayModel`), `DEFAULT_VIDEO_SETTINGS`, `ContentProject.videoSettings` (beside the brief, so changing it puts nothing out of date). `contentProjectService.ts`: `getVideoSettings()`, `setVideoSettings()`, `setMainMoment(projectId, sceneId, on)` (edits the chosen scene plan; no version goes out of date). Miya's scene plan marks `mainMoment` (hook, turning points, call to action); only an explicit `true` counts |
| A Runway clip (content factory phase 2b) | `src/services/contentFactory/runwayClip.ts` — `runwayClipAvailable()` (a saved Runway key, Zero-Cost Mode off, and `paidModels.runwayPricePerSecondUsd` entered in Settings → Approvals → Allow paid models; else the reason), `runwayClipSeconds()` (5 or 10, the shorter that covers the scene), `estimateRunwayUsd()`, `makeRunwayClip()` (`canSpend()` first so nothing is sent past the shared monthly cap, the scene picture as a data URL through `generateRunwayVideo()` approved (the run was approved once through the step's `spend` effect), `gen4_turbo` by default, 1280:720 or 720:1280, waits up to 30 min (a timeout says Runway may still charge), records the cost with `recordSpendUsd()` in `joseSpend.ts` once Runway made the clip). The clip keeps Runway's file name in `<project>/clips/` |
| A local AI clip through ComfyUI (content factory phase 2b) | `src/services/contentFactory/comfyVideoClip.ts` — `framesFor(modelId, seconds)` (n·step+1 frames, at least 9, at most the model's 5 s), `buildVideoWorkflow(modelId, …)` (API-format graphs copied from ComfyUI's official templates `ltxv_image_to_video` (distilled: 8 steps, cfg 1) and `video_wan2_2_5B_ti2v`, with the pinned file names), `makeComfyClip({ root, imageRel, outRel, prompt, seconds, vertical, modelId })` (`freeOllamaGpuMemory()`, upload, `/prompt`, wait up to 15 min, save; `pending` with the job id after that) and `checkComfyClip()`. Backend in `comfyui_proxy.rs`: `comfyui_upload_image` (multipart built by `multipart_image_body()`; the picture through `checked_file`) and `comfyui_save_output` (`view_path()` allows plain output/temp files only; 500 MB cap; `.part` then rename; never over an existing file); both loopback only. `comfyRequest()` is exported from `connectorImageGenerators.js` for this |
| Bundled FFmpeg and the media commands (content factory phase 2a) | `scripts/fetch-ffmpeg.mjs` (`FFMPEG_ASSET`: a pinned BtbN LGPL shared build, SHA-256 checked; keeps `ffmpeg.exe`, `ffprobe.exe`, their DLLs and `LICENSE.txt` in `src-tauri/vendor/ffmpeg/`, bundled as `ffmpeg/`; Windows CI and release jobs only, macOS/Linux use the PATH) and `src-tauri/src/media_tools.rs`: `ffmpeg_path()`/`ffprobe_path()` (bundled first, then PATH), `media_tools_status` (ffmpeg, ffprobe, kokoro, audiocraft, whisper), `content_tts_kokoro` (runs `voice/content/kokoro_speak.py` with Kokoro's own Python: one WAV per line, `full.wav`, each line's start; a line with no words is `pauseSec` of silence), `content_music_generate` (`voice/content/musicgen_generate.py`, 5–300 s; not installed → `{ ok: false, missing: true }`), `content_whisper_words` (Whisper word times on the voice track), `content_stock_search` / `content_download_media` (row below), `content_assemble_video` (`build_assembly_runs()`: one ffmpeg run per scene, the join, then the mix with captions burned in from an SRT by a plain file name; both 16:9 and 9:16; ffprobe checks length (within 1 s of the voice) and streams; three frames each; a version that exists is refused, and a run that fails removes every file it wrote, so Retry can use the same version) and `content_allow_media_folder` (adds the output folder to the asset protocol's scope, which starts empty in `tauri.conf.json`; never a drive root). Since phase 2b a scene may carry `aiClip`/`aiSeconds`: `assembly_segments()` plans the AI clip first (at most the scene's length), then the stock clip, picture or card for the rest (nothing when under 0.05 s); the runs and `segments.txt` come from that one plan, and `missing_input()` checks every input (AI clips too). `runAssemble()` sends only clips the AI clips step made. Paths go through `checked_dir()`/`checked_file()` (inside the workspace, not `.git`). The scripts ship in the installer (`voice/content/*.py`). Do NOT build ffmpeg command lines with a shell or put text on a command line: lines go in a job file, captions in an SRT |

Backend: `content_stock_search` (fixed endpoints; Pexels key in `Authorization`, Pixabay in its query, Coverr `Bearer`) and `content_download_media` (https, the stock hosts only (`allowed_media_host()`), every redirect checked, 300 MB cap, `.part` then rename; counts a Coverr download as Coverr requires) in `media_tools.rs`. | Jose cron scheduler | `src/services/joseSchedulerService.ts` — `createSchedule`, `listSchedules`, `runSchedule(id)` (Run now), `tickScheduler()`, `startScheduler({ runCommand, onResult })`/`stopScheduler`, `nextCronMs()` (minute hour day month weekday; numbers, `*`, `*/n`), `SCHEDULE_PRESETS` (timings, plus built-in agent jobs with a `handler` and `description`). A schedule runs either a command (App.tsx's `runCommand` → `runScheduledCommand()`: Jose answers or plans it, never plan-first and never with a paid model, approvals by effect as anywhere) or a built-in job (`runNovaAnalysis`, Sentinel's review of the week as a request (`askSentinelWeekReview()`, agent phase 4), Echo's weekly tidy-up as a request (`askEchoCleanup()`, agent phase 7; a schedule saved with the old nightly consolidation runs it too), `fetchRssSources`, Maria's weekly review as a request (`askMariaWeeklyRisks()`, agent phase 3) over the last 7 days of receipts and activity; "Nothing to do" when there is none). Each run's outcome is saved as `lastResult` (`done` / `waiting_approval` / `failed` / `skipped`, summary, `details` = each agent's report) and shown on the card in AutomationView's Schedules tab ("Show result"). Runs while the app is open; one that was due while it was closed runs once at start. Until 2026-10-22 nothing ran (QA Bug 67) |

Until 2026-07-10 this port collided with Voice OS (also defaulting to 8765) — whichever started first silently won the bind and the other failed with no UI feedback, which was very likely the root cause of "mobile companion won't pair" reports; Voice OS was moved to 8766 instead (see Voice OS row above). mDNS discovery (`companion_start_discovery`) is opt-in only — not auto-started at boot, only fires when the user clicks "Start Discovery" in Settings; nothing in the UI currently signals this is a required manual step. |

Do NOT create another pairing UI. |
| Boardroom sessions | `src/components/BoardroomChatView.tsx` — real-time multi-agent group chat. Each thread has **Conclude** (Maria\'s governance review: risk level, approval-required flag and summary, saved on the thread as `governance` via `setThreadGovernance()` and to decision memory), **Distribute** (Marcus: "review only, sends nothing", or post to a Slack channel, which waits for your approval in Jose; reports Marcus\'s real result) and **Creative brief** (Miya). Since phase 6a each is a one-step Jose request (`boardroomJoseService.ts`), shown on the Requests board. These replaced the "Boardroom Legacy" tab, removed 2026-10-21 (source in `archive/MIGHTBEUSEDLATER/`); `migrateLegacySessions()` imports any old session not yet imported (by `legacySessionId`, or topic + creation time). It replaced the old session-summary `BoardroomView.tsx` model as of the 2026-07-10 rebuild (12 phases, following the bloome.im-style handoff PDF the user explicitly authorized mid-session). `src/services/boardroomThreadService.ts`: threads/messages persistence (localStorage), `@mention` parsing, cross-thread keyword-overlap recall (`findCrossThreadContext`), acknowledge/confirm state. `src/services/boardroomFacilitatorService.ts`: real per-agent Ollama generation with persona-specific system prompts (`generateAgentResponse`), hedge-phrase low-confidence detection (`detectLowConfidence`), measured model+latency return values. Key safety/UX behaviors: `@mentions` chain across agents up to a hard `MAX_CHAIN_DEPTH=3` cap AND a character-count budget ceiling (`src/services/fanOutBudgetService.ts` — `createFanOutBudgetTracker`/`DEFAULT_FANOUT_CHAR_BUDGET=40000`, added 2026-09-20/21 closing G-T20's budget half in `docs/TRUTH_FIRST_EXECUTION_PLAN.md`; uses total prompt+response character count as a token proxy, the same approximation `streamingService.ts` already uses for its own live counter, since none of the 4 LLM provider paths return real token-usage figures) before auto-escalating (amber "Needs your decision" banner, `kind:'escalation'`); a Stop button halts further chained hops (already-in-flight hop still completes — no true fetch cancellation, `generateOllamaResponse` has no `AbortController` wiring); failed replies render distinct (rose) with a Retry button that reconstructs the call via a stored `retryContext`; escalations get a one-way Acknowledge control; any `approvalRequired`-flagged message (via the same real `classifyMissionRoomRisk` used elsewhere) renders masked behind a "Confirm to reveal" gate — this is a content-exposure gate, not an action-execution gate, since Boardroom's chat has zero connector/execution calls in its code path (grepped and confirmed, not assumed). Legacy `alphonso_boardroom_sessions_v1` sessions auto-migrate via `migrateLegacySessions()`. `docs/BOARDROOM_MODEL_REGISTRY.md` and `docs/BOARDROOM_ROLES.md` are still stale aspirational planning docs (11-seat design incl. "Hermes"/"Kairo", none of which exist) — correction banners kept, historical context only. Deliberately deferred from the original spec: cards (1.5), regenerate/diff view (1.10.3/1.10.4), resource contention handling (1.10.14), voice input (1.10.6), mobile parity (1.10.8) — see `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-10-boardroom-*-phase{1..12}.md` for the full phase-by-phase implementation record and each phase's explicit "NOT in this phase" scope statement. Since Jose phase 7 a thread action's note opens its request on the board (`BoardroomThreadMessage.requestId`, `boardroom-open-board`, the `onOpenJoseBoard` prop). |

Coach Mode opens as a **separate Tauri webview window** (`coachModeService.ts` — `new WebviewWindow('coach', ...)`), not an in-app panel — until 2026-07-10 that window had zero permissions (capabilities/default.json scoped everything to `"windows": ["main"]` only, no entry for `"coach"`), so even when it opened nothing inside it could function; fixed by adding `"coach"` to the windows list. `openCoachWindow()` now actually awaits `tauri://created`/`tauri://error` instead of resolving optimistically regardless of real outcome, and all 4 call sites (`handleToggleCoachMode`, `handleToggleCoachTop`, `minimizeToCoach`, `openAlphonsoDesktopCard` in `CoachContext.jsx`) surface real errors instead of a misleading "active" toast when running inside Tauri. **SessionGuard bridge naming removed 2026-07-23** — `coachInterventionService.ts` now uses `COACH_ENGINE_*` constants and `coachEngineService.ts` provides 11 local detectors (Phase 1: approval theater, late-night approval, repeated pipeline failure, dead-letter graveyard, confidence decay, rubber-stamp approvals, long unbroken session; Phase 2: agent whiplash, boardroom hedge pileup, unused surface area, license wall) with cooldown/dedup, wired via `CoachContext.jsx` on a 2-min interval. **Phase 3 (message styles):** every detector accepts an optional `style: MessageStyle` param (`direct`/`balanced`/`gentle`); 33 hand-written message variants; persisted via `getCoachMessageStyle()`/`setCoachMessageStyle()` in localStorage. Phase 2 prerequisites: license-denial logging in `licenseService.ts` (`getLicenseDenialLog`/`clearLicenseDenialLog`) and skill use tracking in `src/services/skills/skillUse.ts` (`recordSkillUse`/`skillLastUsed`; was the skill packs' invocation log until 2026-10-25). **Phase 4 (optional Ollama-narrative layer):** `generateNarrativeCoachMessage(signal, style)` in `coachEngineService.ts` rewrites a rule-based message into a more natural one-off phrasing via local Ollama (`generateOllamaResponse` from `lib/ollama.ts` — no second Ollama client) — only for `critical`/`warning` severity, gated behind `getCoachNarrativeEnabled()`/`setCoachNarrativeEnabled()` (localStorage, default **off**), silently falls back to the already-displayed rule-based message on any error/timeout/disabled-setting (never surfaces an Ollama error in the coach window). `CoachContext.jsx` shows the rule-based message immediately and swaps in the narrative rewrite only if/when it resolves — it never blocks the intervention on the LLM call. **Phase 5 (settings + history — closes the settings-UI gap Phase 4 deferred):** a new **Coach** section in `SettingsView.tsx` (`CoachSettingsSection`) exposes a master on/off toggle (`getCoachEnabled`/`setCoachEnabled`), the Phase 4 narrative toggle, a snooze control (1h/4h/24h, `setCoachSnoozeHours`/`clearCoachSnooze`), and all 11 per-trigger enable/disable toggles (`isCoachTriggerEnabled`/`setCoachTriggerEnabled`, keyed off `ALL_COACH_TRIGGER_IDS`). `runCoachDetectors()` now checks the master toggle and snooze state first (returns `null` immediately if either blocks it) and skips any individually-disabled trigger. Every fired signal is now persisted to a capped 50-entry ring buffer via the new `src/services/coachHistoryService.ts` (mirrors `agentAuditService.ts`'s exact shape), surfaced in a new `CoachHistoryPanel.tsx` component (last 20 shown, mirrors the `EchoTimeline`/`NovaHistoryChart` UI convention already in `SettingsView.tsx`). |

A developer tool (owner, 2026-10-22): its sidebar entry (`DEVELOPER_ONLY_ITEMS` in `Sidebar.tsx`) and Home card show only in developer mode, the switch in Settings → Desktop & UI (`developerMode`/`setDeveloperMode` props). Developer mode is the existing `operatorMode` (`alphonso_operator_mode_v1`, off by default); do not add a second flag. Desktop E2E: `enterShell({ developerMode: true })`. |
| Agent avatar system | `src/components/AgentAvatar.tsx` + `src/services/agentAvatarService.ts` — per-agent avatar CRUD and rendering. |
| Agent metrics panel | `src/components/AgentMetricsPanel.tsx` + `src/services/agentMetricsService.ts` — success rate, confidence, 7-day trend. |
| Marketing landing page | `src/components/MarketingLandingPage.tsx` — in-app marketing/landing page component. |
| Memory search UI | `src/components/MemorySearch.tsx` + `src/services/searchService.ts` — memory/project search with Ctrl+P shortcut. |
| Memory knowledge graph service | `src/services/memoryGraphService.ts` — relationship layer over `memory_nodes`/`memory_edges` SQLite tables (`src-tauri/src/memory_graph.rs`). `addNode`/`addEdge`/`queryRelated` (one-hop, Phase 1) + `queryRelatedDeep` (multi-hop via `WITH RECURSIVE`, required `maxDepth`/`direction` params, no ceiling, cycle-safe via visited-edge tracking — Phase 2) + `listAllNodes`/`listAllEdges` (whole-graph enumeration, capped 500/1000 — Phase 3). Writers: `unifiedMemoryService.js`'s `pushMemory`, `boardroomThreadService.ts`'s `addThreadMessage` (Phase 1); `hectorResearchService.js`'s `createResearchDraft`, `orchestrationReceiptService.ts`'s `appendOrchestrationReceipt`, `mariaAuditService.ts`'s `runMariaGovernanceAudit` (Phase 2). Read access is open to every agent regardless of writer count. See `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-03-memory-knowledge-graph-design.md` (Phase 1), `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-03-memory-knowledge-graph-phase2-design.md` (Phase 2), and `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-03-memory-knowledge-graph-visual-viewer-design.md` (Phase 3 viewer) for the full design and roadmap. |
| Memory graph visual viewer | `src/components/MemoryGraphViewer.tsx` — 3D force-directed graph (`react-force-graph-3d`) over the whole memory knowledge graph (`listAllNodes`/`listAllEdges`, capped 500/1000). `compact`/`full` size modes, one component not two renderers — compact lives in `SettingsView.tsx`'s Memory section next to `EchoTimeline`, full opens in `Modal`'s new `size="full"` variant, reachable from both `SettingsView.tsx` and `RightPanel.tsx`. Nodes colored by `node_type` (`NODE_TYPE_COLORS`), not by agent — `memory_nodes` has no `created_by` field, only edges do. Clicking a node opens a detail panel listing its direct connections (via the existing one-hop `queryRelated`), clicking a listed connection selects that node without re-fetching graph data. Links are colored by `confidence` via `trustColor()` (translated to real hex through a local `TRUST_FAMILY_HEX` map, since `trustColor()` returns Tailwind color-family names for the `Badge` component, not literal CSS/WebGL colors), and a "Suggest connections" button runs `inferEdges()` over the currently-loaded graph. See `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-03-memory-knowledge-graph-visual-viewer-design.md`. |
| Memory graph inferred-edge scheduler | `src/services/memoryGraphInferenceService.ts` — `startMemoryGraphInferenceScheduler`/`stopMemoryGraphInferenceScheduler`, wired at `App.tsx` boot next to `startFileWatcher`. Runs `memoryGraphService.ts`'s `inferEdges()` (backed by the Rust `memory_graph_infer_edges` command — structural common-neighbor + shared-`created_event` link prediction, no content inspection) over a random 20-node batch every 30 min. The same `inferEdges()` primitive is also called fire-and-forget from `addNode`/`addEdge` on every successful write (scoped to just the touched nodes, cap 5) and from a "Suggest connections" button in `MemoryGraphViewer.tsx` (scoped to the currently-loaded graph, cap 50) — one bounded primitive, three callers, not three separate implementations. See `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-04-memory-knowledge-graph-inferred-edges-design.md`. |
| Memory graph retention + pruning (Phase 4 governance) | `src/services/memoryGraphRetentionService.ts` (policy constants `GRAPH_RETENTION` — 90 d receipts/packets, 180 d boardroom/report/source nodes, 30 d unconfirmed inferred edges, 14 d access protection, 50% abort guard; builds the numbers + live-memory-item list + protected boardroom ids that Rust executes; `previewMemoryGraphCleanup()` is a dry run, `runMemoryGraphCleanup()` refuses unless a non-aborted Preview ran in the last 30 min, `runScheduledCleanupIfDue()` is the opt-in daily pass — OFF by default, cannot be enabled before a Preview, runs after the inference step in `memoryGraphInferenceService.ts`, audits counts only via `appendAgentActivity`) + `src-tauri/src/memory_graph_prune.rs` (`memory_graph_prune`: one SQLite transaction, `dryRun`, aborts if >50% of nodes would go; protects permanent memory items, nodes one hop from them via non-inferred edges, nodes read in the last 14 days, and escalated/acknowledged boardroom threads; a `source` survives while a surviving `research_report` cites it; memory_item nodes whose memory no longer exists are orphans; an unknown memory list protects every memory_item node). Additive schema: `memory_edges.last_seen_at` (inference re-confirmation refreshes it instead of skipping the pair), `memory_nodes.last_accessed_at` (throttled `touchNodes()` from `queryRelated`/`queryRelatedDeep`, one write per node per hour). Only graph rows are ever deleted, never memory items. Do NOT add a second prune path or a second retention vocabulary. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-06-memory-knowledge-graph-phase4-governance-design.md` |
| Memory graph cleanup panel | `src/components/MemoryGraphCleanupPanel.tsx` — Settings → Memory, under the graph viewer: "Preview cleanup" (dry-run report), "Clean up now" (disabled until a fresh non-aborted preview), "Automatic daily cleanup" checkbox (off by default; explains itself if no Preview has run yet) |
| Smart voice button | `src/components/SmartVoiceButton.tsx` — unified voice input; prefers Voice OS WebSocket (Jarvis), falls back to browser SpeechRecognition. Do NOT add another voice button. |
| Session history view | `src/components/SessionHistoryView.tsx` — orchestration session history with search/filter/expand. |
| Orchestrator queue view | `src/components/OrchestratorQueueView.tsx` — live dead-letter queue dashboard with 5s auto-refresh. |
| Pipeline result card | `src/components/PipelineResultCard.tsx` — standardized pipeline result display card. |
| Trust receipt browser | `src/components/TrustReceiptBrowser.tsx` + `src/services/runtimeLedgerService.ts` — durable receipt browser. |
| Dead letter queue view | `src/components/DeadLetterQueueView.tsx` — UI for dead-letter queue inspection and retry. |
| Workflow operations dashboard | `src/components/WorkflowOperationsDashboard.tsx` — workflow operations status and controls. |
| Tool connections panel | `src/components/ToolConnectionsPanel.tsx` + `src/services/toolConnectionService.ts` — tool connectivity status UI. |
| Miya content factory (phase 1) | Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-miya-content-factory-design.md`; plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-22-miya-content-factory-phase1.md`. A project (one idea for one brand) runs as steps: brief → research (Hector `research_virality`, Nova `analysis_market`) → script (Miya `creative_script`) → review (Nova `analysis_content_review`, Maria `audit_content_quality`, needs approval) → scenes (Miya `creative_scenes` + a ComfyUI picture per scene) → thumbnail (Miya `creative_thumbnails` + 3 finished 1280×720 thumbnails) → campaign (Miya `creative_campaign`, Nova `strategy_timing`) → quality (Maria + Sentinel `security_content_check`, needs approval) → publish (Marcus `distribution_schedule_posts`, `publish_or_send`, phase 3a). **Since phase 6b a project runs as one Jose request** (rows below: `contentProjectPlan.ts`, `contentStepRecorder.ts`); Run this step is a one-step request. `STEP_AGENTS` lives in `contentStepAgents.ts` (re-exported by the runner); the action types match each agent's contract prefixes. Files: `src/services/contentFactory/contentTypes.ts` (types, `STEP_ORDER`, `CONTENT_FORMATS`), `contentBrandService.ts` (brands, key `alphonso_content_brands_v1`; `ensureFirstBrand()` imports the old `miya-brand-kit`; threshold default 7, Kokoro voice), `contentProjectService.ts` (projects, key `alphonso_content_projects_v1`; versions capped at 20; out of date = computed from each version's `basedOn`, captured at `startStep`; `recoverInterruptedSteps()`), `contentProjectFiles.ts` (`<output folder>/<brand>/<project>/`, `slugForFolder()` safe for Windows names, `requireOutputRoot()`, project.json and `<step>/v<n>.md`, `loadProjectImage()` via the backend's `read_workspace_image`), `contentStepPrompts.ts` (prompt per action type with the brand, its last 10 lessons, references and honest-copy rules; `parseStepAnswer()` refuses anything incomplete), `contentAgentExecutor.ts` (`executeContentStepAssignment()`, called by Jose's `executeAssignment` for any assignment with `contentStep`; asks the agent's own model through `generateAgentLlmResponse`, once more if unreadable, then fails with "The model's answer could not be read."), `contentStepRunner.ts` (`runContentStep()`), `contentImages.ts` (scene pictures and thumbnails, one at a time; a picture still in ComfyUI is kept `pending` with its `jobId` for `checkPendingPictures()`), `thumbnailRenderer.ts` (`layoutHeadline()`, `drawHeadline()`: gradient, not a box; the brand's lightest colour or white, darkest as outline), `contentQualityGate.ts` (`visiblePieces()` hides below the brand threshold unless "Show everything"; `rejectPiece()` saves a brand lesson and an Echo memory; `retryWithReason()`). No silent fallback: a step with no usable answer fails and says why. Do NOT add a second content pipeline or call a model directly from a component |
| A project as one Jose request (phase 6b) | `src/services/contentFactory/contentProjectPlan.ts` — `contentProjectPlanSteps(project, brand, from?, { only? })`: the Content video template's 11 steps (`research_trends`, `research_market`, `script`, `review_value`, `review_quality` (both `reviewOf: 'script'`, so the agents' review loop runs, 2 rounds), `scenes` (`waitFor: REVIEW_WAIT`, "Approve the review in Miya Studio first."), `thumbnail`, `campaign`, `timing`, `quality`, `security`), each with `contentStep`; `CONTENT_TEMPLATE_STEPS` maps them to factory steps; links to steps not in the plan are dropped. `contentStepRunner.ts`: `runContentProject(projectId, { settings })` (refuses while a step runs; label `PROJECT_LABEL` "Steps set by Miya Studio."; `saveDecisions: false`; saves `project.joseRequestId`), `runContentStep()` (one factory step as a one-step request), `approveContentStep(projectId, step)` (after the review it also carries the request on, `approveRequestStep`). `PlanStep.contentStep` / `PlanStep.waitFor` reach the assignment; the router and the engine's step gate wait on `waitFor` ("Waiting for you: …"); review revisions and paid retries copy `contentStep`. Do NOT run a factory step any other way |
| Saving a content step as it finishes (phase 6b) | `src/services/contentFactory/contentStepRecorder.ts` — `recordContentStepResult({ projectId, step, agent, data, commandId })` → `'partial'` / `'saved'` / `'ignored'`, called by the engine after a content step's output is saved (so a request carried on from the board, Telegram or Carry on saves too): collects each agent's answer (`StepState.pendingAgents`), saves the version when all of the step's agents have answered, writes the folder, makes scenes/thumbnail pictures. `beginContentStep()` (the executor calls it): shows the step running; Scenes starting marks a review still waiting as approved. The executor reads earlier steps from the project when it runs (`priorOutputs()` in `contentStepAgents.ts`) and adds a revision's "Fix what the review found:" to the task |
| A content video from Chat (phase 6b) | `src/services/contentFactory/contentFromChat.ts` — `startContentProjectFromChat(text, settings)`: default brand (`ensureFirstBrand()`), the message (without "Ask Jose:") as the idea, a name of at most 60 characters, then `runContentProject()` in the background; no output folder → `NEEDS_OUTPUT_FOLDER`. `submitToJose()` takes this path for Chat (`source: 'user'`) when the Content video template fits, the request asks for something to be made (`asksToMake()` in `joseTemplates.ts`: make, create, produce, film, shoot, record, edit, script, write, turn … into; agent phase 2, after "a newsletter, a YouTube channel or a podcast?" started a video project) and the plan is not "fresh"; the result has `contentProjectId`, and Chat shows "Open in Miya Studio" (App's `onOpenContentProject` → `selectContentProject()` in `ContentFactoryPanel.tsx`). Other sources still run the template's steps with `CONTENT_VIDEO_STUDIO_NOTE` Since Jose phase 7 Telegram and schedules start a project too (`CONTENT_PROJECT_SOURCES` in `submitToJose.ts`; a schedule's project has `allowPaidModels: false`); other sources still run the template's steps. |
| Only Jose calls the old pipeline (phase 6b) | `src/test/joseEntryPoints.test.js` fails when a file outside `services/jose/`, `joseExecutionEngineService.ts` and `joseCommandRouterService.ts` calls `createJoseCommandRoute(` or `runJoseCommandExecutionPipeline(`. New callers use `submitToJose()`, `answerOrPlan()` or `submitStepsToJose()` |
| The finished video's steps (content factory phase 2a) | `src/services/contentFactory/contentMediaExecutor.ts` — `MEDIA_ACTIONS` and `executeMediaStep()`, called by `executeContentStepAssignment()` for the four steps between Thumbnails and Campaign (`STEP_ORDER` … thumbnail, voice, music, broll, assemble, campaign …; the Content video template has 15 steps; older projects gain the four as not started): **Voice** (Miya, `creative_voice`: each scene's spoken lines (`linesForScenes()`, by where each script part falls) read by Kokoro in the brand's voice through `content_tts_kokoro`, one WAV per scene plus `full.wav`, a scene with nothing to say kept as silence for its planned seconds; Kokoro missing → fails with "Install Kokoro in Runtime Hub."), **Music** (Miya, `creative_music`: her model writes a mood prompt, `freeOllamaGpuMemory()`, then `content_music_generate`; AudioCraft missing → done with `{ skipped }`), **B-roll** (Hector, `research_broll`: per scene `searchStockClips()` then `content_download_media`, with source, author, page and license; no keys → `{ skipped: NO_STOCK_KEYS }`), **Assemble** (Alphonso, `runtime_assemble_video`: per scene the clip, else the scene picture, else a colour card; captions from Whisper's word times (`content_whisper_words`) when installed, else by sentence with a note; the SRT and `content_assemble_video`). Files go in `<project>/audio`, `broll` and `output`, named by version, never overwritten |
| Captions for the finished video (content factory phase 2a) | `src/services/contentFactory/contentCaptions.ts` — `alignCaptions(scriptWords, heard)` (the words always come from the script; Whisper's word times only give the timing: a misheard word keeps the script's spelling, a missed one is timed between its neighbours, an added one is dropped), `captionsFromLines()` (no Whisper: each line's words spread over its time), `toSrt(words, { maxWords: 6, maxSeconds: 2.5 })` (also breaks after a 1 s pause). ffmpeg burns the SRT in from a file |
| AI clips in the Scene Builder (content factory phase 2b) | `src/components/miya/AiClipsPanel.tsx` — the `clips` step's panel: "AI clips for" (Main moments / Every scene / None) and "Clip engine" (Automatic / Local / Runway, plus the Runway model) saved with `setVideoSettings()`; the engine this computer will use (`resolveProjectClipEngine()`) or why none; for Runway "N clips, about $X.XX" (`estimateRunwayUsd()` over the chosen scenes); "Make AI clips" (with the cost for Runway) while the run waits at the clips step (`approveContentStep(projectId, 'clips')`); each clip with a player, its length and cost; a skipped or failed scene's reason; "Check again" for a clip still in ComfyUI (`checkPendingClips()` in `contentMediaExecutor.ts`, using each pending clip's `target`). `SceneBuilderTab.tsx` puts a "Main moment" switch on each scene card (`setMainMoment()`) while the project uses main moments; `ContentFactoryPanel.tsx` passes `clipsWaiting`, `approveClips`, `checkClips` and `onChange` |
| A project's posts (content factory phase 3a) | `src/services/contentFactory/contentPosts.ts` — `buildPosts(project, brand, nowMs)` → `{ posts, notes }`: one `ProjectPost` (in `contentTypes.ts`) per platform and piece (YouTube video from the 16:9 file with the first finished thumbnail, YouTube Short and Instagram/Facebook Reels from the 9:16 file), the project's platforms first, then the brand's (none named → all three, with a note); copy from the campaign step (the Short's own `short` copy); `nextPostTime()`: the brand's next free `postingSlots` time (Brands tab), else Nova's day and time, else tomorrow 18:00, at least 15 minutes ahead, never two posts at one time. `savePosts()`, `updatePost()` (refused once it has gone out, or when already scheduled on YouTube/Facebook: change it there, with the Studio link), `cancelPost()`. Stored on the project (`posts`, through `changeProjectPosts()`) |
| The one publishing path (content factory phase 3a) | `src/services/contentFactory/publishPost.ts` — `publishPost(post, settings, nowMs)` → `{ status, link?, platformId?, reason? }`: YouTube through `uploadYouTubeConnectorVideo()` (already approved; private with `publishAt` when the time is more than 15 minutes ahead, else public now; the thumbnail; `locked_private` with the Studio link when YouTube kept it private; `check` when its state could not be read back), Instagram and Facebook through `publishMetaVideo()` (Instagram now; Facebook scheduled on Meta). Never "posted" unless the platform says so. Files resolve through `outputPath()` in `contentProjectFiles.ts`. Every post goes through here; do NOT add another publish path |
| Scheduled posts (phase 3a) | Marcus's runner in `src/services/marcusExecutionService.ts` is the one scheduler for posts: `scheduleContentPost()` (same post again moves it), `scheduleContentReminder()` (a locked YouTube video: "make it public now" with the Studio link at its time), `cancelScheduledContentPost()`, `postOverdueNow()`, `recoverScheduledPublishes()` (at start: a post cut off mid-send becomes `check`, never sent again blindly; more than 7 days late becomes `ask`), `runScheduledPublishes()` (claims and saves each item before sending, so two passes never send twice; tells you in the app and on Telegram, saying when it went out late), `startPostScheduler()` (App, not the Coach window; every minute). Text items still go through `executeMarcusPublish()`. Store `alphonso_marcus_schedule_v1`. Do NOT use Jose's scheduler for posts |
| The Publish step (content factory phase 3a) | `publish` is the last of `STEP_ORDER` (Marcus, `distribution_schedule_posts`, `publish_or_send`, so it always asks; needs `quality` and `security` in the Content video template, 19 steps; waits with `PUBLISH_WAIT` in `contentProjectPlan.ts`). `src/services/contentFactory/contentPublishExecutor.ts` — `executePublishStep(assignment)`, dispatched from `executeContentStepAssignment()`: only posts you approved in Campaign Studio (`NO_APPROVED_POSTS` otherwise); YouTube and Facebook through `publishPost()` now (the platform schedules them), Instagram to Marcus's runner (`scheduleContentPost()`) or now when its time has passed; a YouTube video kept private gets `scheduleContentReminder()` at its time. Fails only when every post failed. Content Studio's `publishContent()` (`contentCatalystService.js`) also posts through `publishPost()` now: a local video file, or a public image/video address (`PublishablePost.mediaUrl`), and keeps its own approval and ACC report |
| Campaign Studio's Posts list and ad files (phase 3a) | `src/components/miya/PostsPanel.tsx` — "Make the posts list" (`buildPosts()`, with its notes), each post's title (YouTube), caption, hashtags and time editable until it goes out (a scheduled Instagram post is moved in Marcus's runner too), its status with the link (`openExternalUrl()`) or reason, Cancel (also `cancelScheduledContentPost()`), "Post it now" for one more than 7 days late (`postOverdueNow()`), and "Approve and schedule" → `approveContentStep(projectId, 'publish', settings)` in `contentStepRunner.ts` (approves the drafts, then carries the waiting request on at Publish or runs Publish through Jose). `src/services/contentFactory/adCopyExport.ts` — `adCsv()`, `adsForNetwork()`, `writeAdCsv()` → `<project>/campaign/ads-<network>-v<n>.csv` (Meta Ads Manager: Campaign Name, Ad Set Name, Ad Name, Title, Body; Google Ads Editor for Google and YouTube: Campaign, Ad group, (Ad name,) Headline 1, Description 1; quoted per RFC 4180, a value starting =, +, -, @ kept as text). Columns not yet checked against a downloaded template (no ad account here) |

The Posts list shows a post scheduled on a platform read-only and points to the Calendar, which asks first |

Status labels: `POST_STATUS_LABEL` in `contentPosts.ts` |

Keys never reach an error message. Settings: `src/components/MediaStorageSection.tsx` (Connectors → Content & Productivity → Your storage: the fields, "The address includes the bucket in its path", Save storage, Test with each part's result). Errors carry only the provider's own description, never a token or secret. App side: `src/services/signInService.ts` — `signIn(service)` (the app id and secret saved in Settings: `YOUTUBE_CLIENT_ID`/`_SECRET`, `META_APP_ID`/`_SECRET`, `INSTAGRAM_APP_ID`/`_SECRET` in `meta`; missing → says what to fill in), saves every token with `saveConnectorCredential()` and calls `recordSignIn()`; one Page is saved at once, several are returned to choose (`chooseMetaPage()`); `finishPastedSignIn()`; `refreshInstagramIfDue()` (under `INSTAGRAM_RENEW_DAYS` = 10 left; Marcus's daily `checkSignIns()` calls it). `signInExpiry.ts` counts Instagram as 60 days from signing in or renewing, warned 7 days before (`SIGN_IN_NAME`, `signedInAt()`). UI: `src/components/SignInPanel.tsx` — `SignInButton({ service })` (the steps to set up the user's own app with its redirect address, the button, a box to paste the address while waiting, the Page chooser) under YouTube and Meta in Connectors, and `InstagramLoginSection` (Instagram App ID and Secret, Sign in with Instagram, and "Post to Instagram": whichever is set up / through my Facebook Page / through Instagram Login, `setInstagramRoute()`). A shared Obsidian Media app (no app of one's own) is a separate business decision, written up in DEFERRED_WORK |
